0009f44c 			}
0009f451 			else if( renderType < 1.5 )

0009f48d     	        out_FragColor = vec4(0.67, 0.84, 0.9, fAlpha);
0009f4c9 			}
0009f4ce 			else if( renderType < 2.5 )

0009f4f2 				// AA Line.
0009f502 				// With base of pointer at "0,0"
0009f527 				//float fDistFromTargetOnSurface = vInternalPos.z + length;
0009f568 				vec3 vRayFromEye = normalize( vWorld.xyz - vEyePos.xyz );
0009f5a7 				vec3 n = cross( vRayFromEye, vPointerDirDelta.xyz );
0009f5e0 				float rd = dot( n, vPointerPosDelta.xyz - vEyePos.xyz ) / length ( n );
0009f62c 				float oAlpha = 1.05-400.0*abs(rd);
0009f654 				// From https://mathworld.wolfram.com/Line-LineIntersection.html
0009f699 				vec3 x3 = vWorld.xyz;
0009f6b3 				vec3 x4 = vEyePos.xyz;
0009f6ce 				vec3 x1 = vHitPos.xyz;
0009f6e9 				vec3 x2 = vPointerPosDelta.xyz;
0009f70e 				vec3 A = x2 - x1;
0009f724 				vec3 B = x4 - x3;
0009f73a 				vec3 C = x3 - x1;
0009f750 				vec3 C2 = x3 - x2;
0009f767 				vec3 ABCross = cross( A, B );
0009f78a 				// Distance along pointer ray.
0009f7ad 				float s = dot( cross( C, B ), ABCross ) / dot( ABCross, ABCross );
0009f7f5 				// Distance from hand to point on ray.
0009f820 				float s2 = dot( cross( C2, B ), ABCross ) / dot( ABCross, ABCross );
0009f86a 				// Distance along headset ray. (unsued)
0009f896 				//float t =-dot( cross( C, A ), ABCross );
0009f8c6 				if( s < 0.0 ) oAlpha = 0.0;
0009f8e6 				float fLength = length( vHitPos.xyz - vPointerPosDelta.xyz );
0009f928 				float closenessToNear = -1.0 - s2*80.0;
0009f954 				oAlpha = min( oAlpha, closenessToNear );
0009f981 				if( renderLine < 0.5f) {
0009f99e 					oAlpha = 0.f;
0009f9b1 				}
0009f9b8 				// Compute intersection with "ball" (or really flat circle) at intersection.
0009fa09 				// This is faster than a ray-sphere intersection.
0009fa40 				vec3 vecCircleHit = cross( vHitPos.xyz - vEyePos.xyz, vRayFromEye );
